How to use this knowledge base when answering questions about Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki:
- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Baby Chick Chicken Skin
Categories: Epic items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales
[Baby Chick Chicken Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_SKIN_CHICKEN_BABY_CHICK
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Chicken pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The Baby Chick Chicken Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Baby Chick Chicken Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 5, 2021. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and, unlike other Fire Sales, had an unlimited stock, with 3,474 sold.
## Usage
The Baby Chick Chicken Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Chicken Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.
## Gallery
- Baby Chick Chicken Skin
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 3, 2021|Announced the [[Fire Sale]] for the Baby Chick Chicken Skin.|
|Apr 5, 2021|Started the [[Fire Sale]] for the Baby Chick Chicken Skin.|

The Baby Hydra Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be obtained from a past [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Baby Hydra Skin could be purchased from the Spring 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.
## Usage
The Baby Hydra Skin can be applied to a [[Water Hydra Head]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

# Bal Shard
Categories: Epic items, Shards
[Bal Shard]
Type: Shard ([[Attributes]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Shard ID: E15
Families: Cave Dweller ([[Shard Families]])
Skill: Mining
Category: Combat
[Attribute]
Name: Deep Technique ([[Attributes]])
Effect: Each block you mine has a +1%–10% chance to grant 1 extra [[Pristine]] for that block.
Requirement: Hunting XV (15) ([[Hunting]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: BAL
Tooltip: Deep Technique I Each block you mine has a +1%; chance to grant 1 extra  Pristine for that block.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC COMBAT SHARD (ID E15)
The Bal Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Deep Technique]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Bal Shard has a 0.125% (1 in 800 hits) chance to drop from damaging [[Bal]].
### Fusion
A Bal Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bal Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Deep Technique]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Deep Technique ([[Attributes]]); Each block you mine has a +1%–10% chance to grant 1 extra [[Pristine]] for that block.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|

Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Barrel of Riches will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +10% chance for [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] to be more likely to be GOLD. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Barrel of Riches for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.

# Basket of Seeds
Aliases: Basket Of Seeds
Categories: Epic items, Items, Tools, Farming, Container
[Basket of Seeds]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Obtained: [[Anita]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Anita]]
Buy: 2 Silver Medal ([[Medals]]) 30 [[Jacob's Ticket]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BASKET_OF_SEEDS
Tooltip: Ability: Seed Storage LEFT CLICK Place seeds in the basket.; Ability: Farmer's Delight RIGHT CLICK Automatically seed a row of farmland.; EPIC
The Basket of Seeds is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item which allows players to plant seeds in a row of farmland if it has the required seeds in its inventory.
## Obtaining
The Basket of Seeds can be bought from [[Anita]] for 2 [[Medals|Silver Medal]] and 30 [[Jacob's Ticket|Jacob's Tickets]].
## Usage
Item:
Basket of Seeds
[Effects]
[Ability: Seed Storage LEFT CLICK; Place seeds in the basket. Ability: Farmer's Delight RIGHT CLICK; Automatically seed a row of farmland.]
- The storage size of the Seed Storage ability is 54 slots or 3,456 items.
- Despite being called the "Basket of Seeds", it can be used to plant several other crops, including [[Melon Seeds]], [[Pumpkin Seeds]], [[Carrot|Carrots]], [[Potato|Potatoes]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Cactus]], [[Moonflower|Moonflowers]], [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]] and [[Wild Rose|Wild Roses]].
- Each use of Farmer's Delight sows a maximum of 250 crops on [[Grass]], [[Farmland]], [[Sand]] or [[Jungle Log]]. Crops are consumed upon use. If an obstacle appears on the row after using Farmer's Delight, the non-sown crops will not be refunded, even if that new obstacle is a crop of the same type.
## Trivia
- The addition of the Basket of Seeds alongside various other new farming items sold by [[Anita]] was hinted by 12[[Ranks|[ADMIN]]] Minikloon through a video on his YouTube channel.
- Jumping on farmland while the player holds a Basket of Seeds in their hand used to not turn it back into dirt. However, this seems to be no longer the case. It's unknown exactly when this was changed.
- Unlike the [[Builder's Wand]], the Basket of Seeds cannot use items in the player's inventory.
- The Basket of Seeds cannot be put in the [[Ender Chest]] or [[Backpack|Backpacks]].
- Attempting to place seeds in an invalid location results in the message "This seed can't be placed here!".
## Bugs
- Sometimes, the Basket of Seeds will have seeds in it, but when you try to use it, the player will get a message saying "Seed inventory empty!" This may be caused by the seeds being vanilla seeds, which the Basket of Seeds does not work with. The vanilla seeds could have been obtained by crafting them in the vanilla 2x2 pattern. They can be turned into Hypixel SkyBlock seeds by dropping them and picking them back up. Vanilla seeds do not have the [[Rarity]] displayed on the tooltip.
## Demonstration
Basket of Seeds Demonstration
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Basket of Seeds as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 4, 2020|The Basket of Seeds is shown on 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) Minikloon's YouTube channel.|
|Nov 6, 2020|0.9.11|Added Basket of Seeds with support for [[Seeds]], [[Melon Seeds]], [[Pumpkin Seeds]], Carrots ([[Carrot]]), and Potatoes ([[Potato]]).|
|Apr 5, 2021|The Basket of Seeds has been temporarily disabled|
|Apr 8, 2021|The Basket of Seeds has been re-enabled.|
|Apr 8, 2021|The Basket of Seeds has been temporarily disabled, again.|
|May 25, 2021|The Basket of Seeds right-click ability has been re-enabled.|
|Jun 1, 2021|The Basket of Seeds has been re-enabled|
|Feb 14, 2023|0.18|Added support for [[Sugar Cane]], [[Cocoa Beans]], and [[Cactus]] to the Basket of Seeds.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Fixed an exploit with the Basket of Seeds.|
|Dec 15, 2025|0.24|Added support for [[Sunflower]], [[Moonflower]], and [[Wild Rose]] to the Basket of Seeds.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Basket of Seeds as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# BeeDazzled Bee Skin
Categories: Epic items, Pet Skins, Fire Sales
[BeeDazzled Bee Skin]
Type: Pet Skin ([[Pet Skins]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Bee pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The BeeDazzled Bee Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The BeeDazzled Bee Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 24, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,500 purchases.
## Usage
The BeeDazzled Bee Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Bee Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Beeheemoth Shard

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Syphonable: ✔ Yes
ID-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✕ No
Chameleon-Fusable ([[Attribute Fusion]]): ✔ Yes
[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: BEEHEEMOTH
Tooltip: Lathering Greed I Gain +10% more Honeycomb from hunting mobs spawned from Trees lathered with Honeycomb.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E52)
The Beeheemoth Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Lathering Greed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Beeheemoth Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Beeheemoth]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]].
### Fusion
A Beeheemoth Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Queen Bee Shard]]
- Second: [[Lunar Moth Shard]]
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Beeheemoth Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Lathering Greed]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Lathering Greed ([[Attributes]]); Gain +10%–100% more [[Honeycomb]] from hunting mobs spawned from Trees lathered with Honeycomb.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Beeheemoth Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
[Data from SkyShards.]
## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Beeheemoth Shard has the following description:
- The Beeheemoth is thousands of times larger than it ought to be, though nobody quite knows why.

A Beheaded Horror is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item.
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
A Beheaded Horror is an [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] drop from Tier III or higher [[Revenant Horror|Revenant Horrors]]. It is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer LVL V (5)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier III: 0.08% (1 in 1,250)
- Tier IV: 0.16% (1 in 625)
- Tier V: 0.14% (1 in 714.29)
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Mask]]|Zombie Slayer LVL VII (7) ([[Zombie Slayer]])|1x [[Revived Heart]] [[Beheaded Horror]] 1x Beheaded Horror 32x [[Enchanted String]] 128x (2 stacks (128 items total)) [[Revenant Viscera]]|Recipe: [[Revenant Viscera]] x32 ×4 + Beheaded Horror + [[Enchanted String]] x32 + [[Revived Heart]] → [[Reaper Mask]]|

## Obtaining
### Crafting
The Berserker Armor requires 13 [[Enchanted Red Sand Cube|Enchanted Red Sand Cubes]], 160 (2 stacks plus 32) [[Magma Chunk|Magma Chunks]] and 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Burning Eye|Burning Eyes]] to craft.

[Big Brain Zombie Skin]

Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Zombie pets.; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; EPIC COSMETIC
The Big Brain Zombie Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Big Brain Zombie Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 23, 2023. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Big Brain Zombie Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Zombie Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Bigfoot's Bola
Aliases: Bigfoot's Lasso
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mob Drops, Crafting ingredients, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Bigfoot's Bola]
Type: [[Precursor Relic]]
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Tooltip Text: Hard to throw, even harder to catch.
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Shop]
Sell: 50,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GIANT_FRAGMENT_BIGFOOT
Tooltip: Precursor Relic; Hard to throw, even harder to catch.; Right-click to view recipes!; EPIC DUNGEON ITEM
Bigfoot's Bola is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Precursor Relic]]. It can be used to upgrade [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] into the [[Scylla]], and is also used to upgrade [[Wither Armor]] into [[Maxor's Armor]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Bigfoot's Bola has a 50% chance to be dropped from [[Bigfoot]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] (Summoned by [[The Watcher]]). This is not affected by [[Magic Find]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv485-895]|[[Bigfoot (Watcher)]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Maxor's Helmet ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|[[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8 Bigfoot's Bola Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|Maxor's Chestplate ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|[[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8 Bigfoot's Bola Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|Maxor's Leggings ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|[[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8 Bigfoot's Bola Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|Maxor's Boots ([[Maxor's Armor]])|None|[[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8 Bigfoot's Bola Wither Boots ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]]/[[Necron's Armor]] → [[Maxor's Armor]]|
|[[Scylla]]|None|[[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8 Bigfoot's Bola One of the following: [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]] [[Hyperion]] [[Astraea]] [[Valkyrie]]|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)]]/[[Hyperion]]/[[Astraea]]/[[Valkyrie]] → [[Scylla]]|
|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|None|1x [[Felthorn Reaper]] [[Bigfoot's Bola]] 8x Bigfoot's Bola|Recipe: Bigfoot's Bola ×8 + [[Felthorn Reaper]] → [[Felthorn Reaper]]|

The Bingbow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]].

The Bingbow may be offered when talking to [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] on a [[Bingo|Special Bingo]] profile after completing a set number of goals.

Bow:
Bingbow [[Damage]]: +210 [[Strength]]: +100
[Effects]
[Your arrows have a 20% chance to bounce to another target after it hits something. (max 2 bounces)]
The Bingbow is a shortbow, meaning it can fire arrows instantly, without needing to charge. The maximum rate of fire is determined by the player's [[Attack Speed]].

# Bingo Booster
Categories: Epic items, Items
[Bingo Booster]
Type: Pet Item ([[Pet Items]])

Source: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_BOOSTER
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Doubles the efficiency of the Lucky Looting perk of your Bingo Pet.; Right-click on your summoned Bingo pet to give it this item!; * Soulbound * EPIC PET ITEM
The Bingo Blaster is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that grants the [[Bingo Pet]]'s Lucky Looting perk double efficiency.
## Obtaining
The Bingo Booster may be offered when talking to [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] on a [[Bingo|Special Bingo]] profile after completing a set number of goals.
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Bingo Pet]] whilst holding a Bingo Booster will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], doubling the number of collection items obtained by Lucky Looting. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would replace the Bingo Booster, and remove the perks it gave.
## Trivia
- As it is [[Soulbound]], the Bingo Booster cannot be transferred from a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile to any other profile.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 25, 2023|Added Bingo Booster.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Bingo Booster requires completing 12 goals in the 2nd Extreme Bingo ([[Bingo]]).|

Next →: None

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 200 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]]); 1 [[Bingo Artifact]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BINGO_RELIC
Tooltip: Accessory Power: +12; B.I.N.G.O.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Bingo Relic is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]], purchasable from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]].
## Obtaining
Bingo Relic can be purchased from [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 1 [[Bingo Artifact]] and 200 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]] once the player has reached Ⓑ Bingo Rank IV (4).
Bingo Relic can also be obtained as a MAJOR reward from [[Raffle of the Century]].
## Trivia
- Alongside [[Hegemony Artifact]] and [[Hocus-Pocus Cipher]] (outside of the rift) it does not grant any effects, which means it can only grant boosts through [[Powers|Accessory Powers]].
- The lore of the item is a reference to the children's song BINGO, where the letters of the word are sung to spell out the name of a farmer's dog.
  - Furthermore, when playing the game Bingo, columns are often distinguished by those letters as opposed to A, B, C...
## History

|Nov 28, 2022|Added Bingo Relic.|
|Dec 1, 2022|0.17|Bingo Relic becomes obtainable.|

Tooltip: Breaking Power 7; Damage: +30; Mining Speed: +1,500; Mining Fortune: +100; A powerful tool that doesn't seem to break.; This item can be reforged!; * Soulbound * EPIC PICKAXE
The Bingonimbus 2000 is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]].
## Obtaining
The Bingonimbus 2000 may be offered when talking to [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo]] or [[Chuckleton]] on a [[Bingo|Special Bingo]] profile after completing a set number of goals.
## Usage
Pickaxe:
Bingonimbus 2000 [[Damage]]: +30 [[Breaking Power]]: +7 [[Mining Speed]]: +1500 [[Mining Fortune]]: +100
## Trivia
- The Bingonimbus 2000 is a reference to both the [[Pickonimbus 2000]] and the Nimbus 2000 from the Harry Potter franchise.
- As it is [[Soulbound]], the Bingonimbus 2000 cannot be transferred from a [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profile to any other profile.

## Trivia
- Full Set Bonus: Blazing Aura does not work on [[Private Island]].
- This set may not suit players who are looking for a strength boost that they can get out of [[Strong Dragon Armor]], which is both cheaper and easier to obtain.
  - However, its upgrade [[Frozen Blaze Armor]], when combined with a [[Blaze Pet]], remains competitive against even [[Crimson Armor|Fiery Crimson Armor]] and [[Necron's Armor]], only really falling short once better pets are usable.
  - As the upgrade is far cheaper than the cost of a high-level [[Blaze Pet]], most players pass over the base Blaze Armor in favor of the upgrade.
- Blaze Armor will deal damage to invisible [[Sneaky Creeper]] without removing their invisibility.
- The helmet's texture is based on Mob D, a mob concept from a 2017 Minecon poll.

# Block Zapper
Aliases: Zapper
Categories: Epic items, Items, Tools, Other Tools, Bits Shop
[Block Zapper]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Bits Shop]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 5,000 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLOCK_ZAPPER
Tooltip: Ability: Grand... Zapper? RIGHT CLICK Right-Click a block to zap all connected blocks of the same type.; EPIC
Block Zapper is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item that can be purchased via [[Elizabeth]]'s [[Bits Shop]] for 5,000 [[Bits]]. It can be used on [[Private Island|Private Islands]] to zap connected blocks, placing them in the player's inventory.
## Obtaining
A Block Zapper can be purchased from [[Elizabeth]] for 5,000 [[Bits]].
## Usage
Item:
Block Zapper
[Effects]
[Ability: Grand... Zapper? RIGHT CLICK; Right-Click the face of a block to zap all connected blocks of the same type.]
When a block on the user's Private Island is right-clicked, the Block Zapper will remove up to 164 connected blocks of the same type from the world and place it in the user's inventory. After zapping, a chat message will inform the user of how many blocks were zapped. Next to it, an Undo button allows the player to undo the previous zap.
The Block Zapper has several limitations:
- It cannot zap tile entities such as Chests and Crafting Tables.
- It cannot zap crops or blocks under crops.
- It will not fully zap all hardened clay blocks and can only zap one at a time.
- It cannot zap Prismarine type blocks.
  - This may be because of issues related to Mithril.
- It can only zap blocks the player has placed.
  - Blocks placed by Minions cannot be zapped.
  - Blocks generated naturally (such as Cobblestone from Lava and Water) cannot be zapped.
- The item is considered fatigued and won't work if the use 20 uses ago was under 8 minutes ago.
  - This cooldown is for all block zappers, so you cannot use multiple to bypass the cooldown.
- It will destroy blocks in a straight line, then loop back in a snake-like pattern until the 164 block limit is reached or all connected blocks are destroyed.
  - This is useful for building farms; by building a square of dirt 164 blocks wide (ideally using the [[Builder's Wand]]), then using the Block Zapper, the player can create perfect water trenches without the need to dig blocks manually.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Zapping blocks|Zapped # blocks! UNDO|
|Undoing block zap|Unzapped # blocks away!|
|Undo command expired|The undo on this zap has expired!|
|On cooldown; Before 0.19.9 update|Your zapper is temporarily fatigued!|
|Using on a Public Island|You cannot use this item on a public island!|
|Inventory space is full|You don't have enough room in your inventory to hold all the zapped blocks!|
|Zapping block that was not placed by a player|You can only zap blocks you've placed!|
|Zapping block that cannot be zapped|Can't zap this!|
## Trivia
- If you zap [[Mycelium]] using a Block Zapper, it will turn into dirt.
- When the item first came out, the Block Zapper could be abused to farm crops extremely efficiently and quickly. This has since been patched.
- Using a Block Zapper on upper-half Red Sandstone Slabs used to produce a [[Null]] item.
## Showcase
Block Zapper Showcase
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Added Block Zapper.|
|Feb 16, 2021|Can no longer zap crops.|
|Mar 18, 2021|On non-ironman profiles, can now only zap blocks the player has placed, making it unable to zap Cobblestone in a generator.|
|May 21, 2021|Added 7 minute fatigue cooldown after 20 uses.|
|Nov 8, 2021|The Block Zapper will now put certain items into the player's [[Sacks]], instead of their inventory.|
|Feb 22, 2023|Fixed being able to use the Block Zapper on custom Garden presets to "transfer" blocks out.|
|May 11, 2023|0.18.4|Fixed this item occasionally not showing a message.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Removed cooldown from Block Zapper.|

# Blooming Thorns
Aliases: Thorny
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Craftable items
[Blooming Thorns]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Thorny
Applied To: [[Equipment]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLOOMING_THORNS
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Thorny reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Overbloom  Farming Fortune; Thorny Bonus Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Blooming Thorns is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Thorny]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]], which grants [[Overbloom]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|2x [[Thornshade]] 4x [[Thorn Fragment]] 18x [[Cactus Flower]] 18x [[Compacted Wild Rose]]|Recipe: [[Cactus Flower]] x6 ×3 + [[Thorn Fragment]] x2 ×2 + [[Thornshade]] x2 + [[Compacted Wild Rose]] x6 ×3 → Blooming Thorns|
## Usage
### Reforging
Blooming Thorns can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Thorny]] reforge, to grant [[Overbloom]] and [[Farming Fortune]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Thorny]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Thorny Bonus, which grants 0.1 [[Overbloom]] per tier of [[Thorns]] enchantment on all of player's equipped armor pieces.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +0.25 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+4 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +0.5 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+6 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +0.75 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+8 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+10 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1.25 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+12 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1.5 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|+14 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]]) +1.85 Bloom ([[Overbloom]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|Thorny Bonus; Grants an additional +0.1 Overbloom per tier of Thorns on all of your armor pieces.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Trivia
- This Reforge Stone was designed to give the [[Thorns]] Enchantment a purpose, as it was underused before, especially considering the addition of [[Thorns|Thorns IV]].
- The stats granted by the special ability are shown as if they were given by the reforge by default.

The Blossom Set is a combination of four pieces of Blossom [[Equipment]] which are all obtained from [[The Garden|SkyMart]] in [[The Garden]].
## Usage
Equipment:
|Necklace|Cloak|
|-|-|
|[[Blossom Necklace]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Farming Fortune]]: +7 Piece Bonus: Florist; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|[[Blossom Cloak]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Farming Fortune]]: +7 Piece Bonus: Florist; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|
|Belt|Bracelet|
|[[Blossom Belt]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Farming Fortune]]: +7 Piece Bonus: Florist; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|[[Blossom Bracelet]]; [[Health]]: +15 [[Farming Fortune]]: +7 Piece Bonus: Florist; Complete Garden Visitor Offers to gain [[Farming Fortune]].|
[Total]
[Blossom Set Health: +60 Farming Fortune: +28]
### Bonus Farming Fortune
Pieces of this set give a bonus to the player's [[Farming Fortune]]. This bonus is granted independently for every piece a player wears.
The Blossom Set's Florist bonus grants the player additional [[Farming Fortune]] depending on the amount of [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] they have served.
|Bonus|Visitors Served|
|-|-|
|1.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|1|
|3.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|5|
|4.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|10|
|6.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|20|
|7.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|50|
|9.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|75|
|10.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|100|
|12.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|150|
|13.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|250|
|15.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|500|
|16.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|750|
|18.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|1,000|
|19.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|1,500|
|21.0 [[Farming Fortune]]|2,000|
|22.5 [[Farming Fortune]]|2,500|
## Notes
- It is important to note that the [[Farming Fortune]] from this equipment's Piece Bonus doesn't show in the items' stats, which might give the false impression of it giving up to 22.5 , less than it actually gives.
- Unlike other equipment with upgradeable stats, the Blossom Set's [[Farming Fortune]] bonus milestone scales with the player's total Visitor milestone rather than the visitors served while the equipment is worn.

The Blue Chick Chicken Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Blue Chick Chicken Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on April 26, 2025. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 3,000 purchases.
## Usage

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No

[Metadata]
Item ID: ATTRIBUTE_SHARD
Shard Item ID: BLUE_CRAB
Tooltip: Outstanding Fisher I Treasure catches are +1% more likely to be OUTSTANDING.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC WATER SHARD (ID E23)
The Blue Crab Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Outstanding Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Blue Crab Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Crustacean Family
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Treasure Fish Family
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Blue Crab Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Outstanding Fisher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Outstanding Fisher ([[Attributes]]); Treasure catches are +1%–10% more likely to be OUTSTANDING.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Blue Crab Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Blue Crab Shard has the following description:
- A single Blue Crab can carry millions of her eggs at once.

There's more...; Adds +4 Damage bonus to the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio.; Though it may not be bluer visually, it is in spirit.; Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Bluertooth Ring is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Bluetooth Ring]] 2x [[Blue Goblin Egg]] 16x [[Blue Shark Tooth]] 128x [[Blue Ring]]|Recipe: [[Blue Ring]] x32 ×4 + [[Blue Goblin Egg]] ×2 + [[Blue Shark Tooth]] x8 ×2 + [[Bluetooth Ring]] → Bluertooth Ring|
## Usage
Accessory:
Bluertooth Ring [[Intelligence]]: -2 [[Speed]]: -3
[Effects]
[Deal +2 Damage if there are at least 6 players on the island you're on.; Adds +4 Damage bonus to the Blazetekk™ Ham Radio Blazetekk™ Ham Radio.]
## Trivia
- This along with the [[Green Jerry Talisman|Jerry]] line of [[Accessories]] are the only [[Accessories]] that give negative [[Stats]].
- The item description is most likely in reference to the item rarity, [[Rarity|EPIC]], compared to its predecessor, the [[Bluetooth Ring]], which is [[Rarity|RARE]]. This means that the Bluertooth Ring has an item name with a text color of purple rather than blue.

## Usage
Sword:
Bone Reaver [[Damage]]: +235 [[Strength]]: +90 [[Swing Range]]: +0.5
[Effects]
[Deals +1% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) monsters for every 1% of your missing health. Ability: Reaving Strike RIGHT CLICK; Slash in a huge arc, dealing 125% melee damage to all enemies hit!; Charges: 4 / 5s]
The Bone Reaver, being a [[Longswords|Longsword]], grants +0.5 [[Swing Range]]. It also deals +1% more damage to [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs per 1% [[Health]] the player is missing.
The Bone Reaver's Reaving Strike allows the wielder to slash in an arc: this slash deals +125% of the wielder's melee damage to all mobs that are hit. This ability has 4 charges: each charge recharges every 5s.

|[[Felthorn Reaper]]|None|1x [[Fel Skull]] [[Bone Reaver]] 1x Bone Reaver 5x [[Fel Rose]]|Recipe: [[Fel Rose]] ×5 + [[Fel Skull]] + Bone Reaver → [[Felthorn Reaper]]|

The Bonzo Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Catacombs Explorer]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Reward Chest
A Bonzo Shard can be obtained from [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] in the [[Catacombs]].
|Catacombs Floor ([[Catacombs]])|Chest ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Cost; (Chest + Added)|Average Chance; (No Bonuses)|Average Chance; (S+ (Score above 300 points.) ([[Dungeon Score]]) Max Bonuses ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]]))|Quality ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|Weight ([[Dungeon Reward Chest]])|

### Fusion
A Bonzo Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].

## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Bonzo Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Catacombs Explorer]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Catacombs Explorer ([[Attributes]]); Grants +1%–10% more Catacombs XP.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Bonzo Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Lore
When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Bonzo Shard has the following description:
- Though a novice, Bonzo is particularly eager to prove himself as worthy to his peers. Guarding the very first dungeon floor, he seeks to snuff things out before they become a problem - and has fun doing it!

# Book of Stats
Aliases: Book Of Stats
Categories: Epic items, Items, Events, Bingo
[Book of Stats]
Type: Cosmetic
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 5 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOOK_OF_STATS
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Tracks the amount of kills and crops harvested when applied to its respective tool.; EPIC COSMETIC
Book of Stats is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] cosmetic that shows the amount of kills a [[Weapons|Weapon]] has or the amount of crops a [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] has harvested.
## Obtaining
It can be bought from the [[Bingo|Bingo Shop]] for 5 Bingo Points.
## Usage
A Book of Stats can be applied to any [[Weapons|Weapon]], including [[Swords]] and [[Bows]], or [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]]. It may be appliable to Wands, Support items and miscellaneous items (E.g. [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]). Once applied, it will start tracking the Weapon's kills or Farming Tool's total crops harvested, displaying in its tooltip.
## History

|Dec 1, 2021|Book of Stats becomes obtainable.|

# Brain Food
Categories: Epic items, Items, Mob Drops, Permanent Consumables
[Brain Food]
Type: Consumable Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BRAIN_FOOD
Tooltip: Consumable; Consume this item to permanently gain +1 Tracking.; You can only consume 5 of this item before the information overload starts to cause you physical pain.; Is it called brain food because it's good for the brain, or because it's made of them?; Right-click to consume!; EPIC
Brain Food is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Consumables|Permanent Consumable]]. It grants the player +1 [[Tracking]] up to 5 times, for a maximum of +5 [[Tracking]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv349]|Empyrean Sphinx ([[Sphinx]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.5%|
|[Lv315]|Exalted Sphinx ([[Sphinx]])|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.25%|
## Usage
Brain Food can be consumed up to 5 times. Each Brain Food eaten permanently grants the player +1 [[Tracking]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|First consumption|BRAINLESS! You don't feel all that different, though you do feel a bit more confident in your times tables...|
|Second consumption|BRAINY! A variety of "fun" facts swirl around in your skull. Did you know these before?|
|Third consumption|BRAINPOWER! You look up the sky, and the constellations begin to make sense. Voiceless, they speak to you.|
|Fourth consumption|BRAINROT! Who is the Sphinx to be questioning you, anyway? The Sphinx cannot think like YOU can.|
|Fifth consumption|BRAINFREEZE! Every. single. digit. of pi, etched into your brain. You are the first to have reached the end. Sit down for a while and ruminate in your supreme intellect.|
|Attempting to consume more than 5|You've already consumed the maximum amount of this item!|
## Trivia
- Sphinx in many stories give riddles, testing heroes' wit, which explains why they're the source of Brain Food.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Oct 16, 2025|Added Brain Food.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Nov 4, 2025|0.23.6|Added Brain Food.|

# Brown Bandana
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pet items, Pets

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BROWN_BANDANA
Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet Items can boost pets in many powerful ways! A pet can only hold one Pet Item, but you can swap it at any time!; The pet must be visible to apply the item!; Gain +0.2 Bonus Pest Chance for every Pest Bestiary tier unlocked.; Right-click on your summoned pet to give it this item!; EPIC PET ITEM
Brown Bandana is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] that can be purchased from [[Talbot]] for 200 [[Pelts]]. It grants +0.2 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] for every Pest Bestiary tier unlocked.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
A Brown Bandana can be purchased from [[Talbot]] for 200 [[Pelts]].
## Usage
Right-clicking a [[Pets|Pet]] whilst holding a Brown Bandana will equip it to the [[Pets|Pet]], granting it +0.2 [[Bonus Pest Chance]] for every [[Pests|Pest]] [[Bestiary]] tier unlocked. If another [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] were equipped on the [[Pets|Pet]], it would refund the Brown Bandana for 250,000 [[Coins]] and remove the perks it gave.
The maximum achievable bonus as of SkyBlock 0.24.4 is +45 [[Bonus Pest Chance]].
## Notes
- [[Zombuddy]] and [[Timestalk Clone]] bestiary does not count towards the Brown Bandana's bonus.
- Despite being classified as the [[Pest (Mob Type)|Pest]] mob type, [[Ant]] and [[Queen Ant]] bestiary does not count towards the Brown Bandana's bonus.
## History

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Brown Bandana.|

Bundle of Magma Arrows is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained as an [[Drop Chance|3Occasional]] drop from [[Inferno Demonlord]] Tier II+.
## Obtaining
A Bundle of Magma Arrows can be obtained from Tier II or higher Inferno Demonlords if the player has [[Blaze Slayer|Blaze Slayer I (1)]].
Drop Chances:
- Tier II: 8.47% (1/11.8)
- Tier III: 8.11% (1/12.33)
- Tier IV: 7.62% (1/13.12)
## Usage
If the player has [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]], a Bundle of Magma Arrows can be consumed to obtain 256 [[Arrows|Magma Arrows]]. Arrows obtained are directly added to [[Quiver]]. These arrows are especially useful for Terminator users.

# Burrowing Spores
Aliases: Burrowing Spore, Rooted
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones
[Burrowing Spores]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Rooted
Applied To: [[Equipment]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXIV (24) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BURROWING_SPORES
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Rooted reforge when combined with Equipment.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Farming Fortune; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 24.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
Burrowing Spores is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] which can apply the [[Reforging|Rooted]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Equipment]], which grants [[Health]] and [[Farming Fortune]].
## Obtaining
Burrowing Spores have a 0.0003% (1 in 350,000) chance to drop when breaking a [[Mushroom]] while on [[The Garden]]. Burrowing Spores also has a 0.2% (1 in 500) chance to be found as a Harvest Bounty in the [[Greenhouse]].
Being a [[Rare Crops|RARE CROP]], the drop rate of Burrowing Spores is affected by [[Overbloom]].
## Usage
### Reforging
Burrowing Spores can be applied to [[Equipment]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Rooted]] reforge, to grant [[Health]] and [[Farming Fortune]], which scale with the equipment's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXIV (24)]] and can be done with any equipment, regardless of the piece. Equipment that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Rooted]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+2 HP ([[Health]]) +6 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+5 HP ([[Health]]) +9 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+8 HP ([[Health]]) +12 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+11 HP ([[Health]]) +15 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+14 HP ([[Health]]) +18 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+17 HP ([[Health]]) +21 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|+20 HP ([[Health]]) +25 Frm.Frt ([[Farming Fortune]])|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Caiman Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Alligator Shard]]
- Second: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Combat Category
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Caiman Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Echo of Ruler]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Echo of Ruler ([[Attributes]]); Your Ruler Attributes are +2%–20% stronger.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].

The Cavernshade Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Cavern Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Fusion
A Cavernshade Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]]. For [[Attribute Fusion|Special Fusion]], the following input conditions must be met:
- First: [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher and Cave Dweller Family
- Second: [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher and Cave Dweller Family
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Cavernshade Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Cavern Wisdom]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Cavern Wisdom ([[Attributes]]); Grants +0.5–5 [[Mining Wisdom]].
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Cavernshade Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

When viewed in the [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] or [[Bazaar]], the Cavernshade Shard has the following description:

# Charminizer
Categories: Epic items, Items
[Charminizer]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Combat Level ([[Combat]]) Requirement: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes (+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]))
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Mage Emissary]]
Buy: 100,000 [[Coins]] 1,000x (15 stacks plus 40 (1,000 in total)) [[Mushroom Spore]]
Sell: 60,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHARMINIZER
Tooltip: Can be used on Exe, Wai or Zee Atoms to charm them into only following you.; ❣ Requires Combat Skill 22.; EPIC
The Charminizer is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item used to make an [[Exe]], [[Wai]], or [[Zee]] follow the player.
## Obtaining
The Charminizer can be bought from the [[Mage Emissary]] for 100,000 [[Coins]] and 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Mushroom Spore|Mushroom Spores]], if the player is allied with the Mage [[Factions|Faction]].
## Usage
Right clicking on an [[Exe]], [[Wai]], or [[Zee]] with a Charminizer in hand will charm the atom into following the player. Multiple atoms can be charmed at once, and will float towards the player if they move far enough away. This can be used to lure 2 atoms of the same type together, causing them to combine into another type of atom.
## History

|Jan 30, 2026|Added Charminizer to [[Museum]].|

# Cheetah Talisman
Categories: Epic items, Accessories
[Cheetah Talisman]
Type: Accessory ([[Accessories]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Stats: [[Speed]]: 3
[Upgrades]
← Previous: [[Lynx Talisman]]
Next →: None
[Item Properties]
Upgradeable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Solo)
[Shop]
Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHEETAH_TALISMAN
Tooltip: Speed: +3; Accessory Power: +12; * Soulbound * Works while in Accessory Bag!; EPIC ACCESSORY
The Cheetah Talisman is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Accessories|Accessory]] that is obtained by completing any three races in the [[Dungeon Hub]] with a score of 4/4.
## Obtaining
### Dungeon Hub Race
The Cheetah Talisman can be obtained by completing any three races in the [[Dungeon Hub]] with a score of 4/4. Obtaining the talisman will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"I'm fast as heck boy!!" Achievement]].
Once obtained, it can be repurchased from [[Rusty]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Accessory:
Cheetah Talisman [[Speed]]: +3
## Tips
- The easiest way to get this talisman is by completing 4/4 of all three races with Anything and No Runback.
- The races can be easily completed with the [[Young Dragon Armor]] and at least 350 [[Speed]], paired with a [[Grappling Hook]] or [[Aspect of the End]].
- Another good way is when you get to the end of the race, throw an ender pearl, and make sure it lands after you get teleported back to Guildford and step on the start pressure plate again.
  - For the Giant Mushroom Race and Crystal Core Race, you may have to time this more precisely by throwing the ender pearl a few seconds after stepping on the end race pressure plate, as there is a delay before you get teleported back. Alternatively, you can use the cancel race button to teleport back faster.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Cheetah Talisman.|
|Apr 25, 2022|Cheetah Talisman was added to [[Rusty]]'s shop and is now Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Nov 28, 2023|0.19.8|Changed from Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) to [[Soulbound]].|

# Chorus Fruit
Aliases: Chorus
Categories: Epic items, Items in Sacks, Items, Mutations
[Chorus Fruit]
Type: Mutation
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Medium Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]]); Large Mutations Sack ([[Mutations Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CHORUS_FRUIT
Tooltip: This tricky plant has a mind of its own, teleporting to a new spot every time you think you've found it.; Analyze this Mutation at the Crop Analyzer in Jake's Underground Lab.; EPIC MUTATION
A Chorus Fruit is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Mutations|Mutation]].
## Obtaining
A Chorus Fruit can grow in the [[Greenhouse]] if there are 5 [[Chloronite]] and 3 [[Magic Jellybean]] within 1 block of [[End Stone]].
While growing, a Chorus Fruit does not need water and has 12 Growth Stages. When placed near another crop, it increases [[Farming]] experience by 30% and reduces yield by -20%.
Chorus Fruit teleports as it grows, converting blocks into [[End Stone]]. Chorus Fruit will usually not teleport onto blocks that already contain a crop or [[Mutations|Mutation]]; however, if it is forced to do so, it will destroy that plant in the process.
## Crop Drops
When harvested, Chorus Fruits also drop 1,050 (16 stacks plus 26) [[Potato]], 1,225 (19 stacks plus 9) [[Carrot]], 1,400 (21 stacks plus 56) [[Sugar Cane]], and +5,600 [[Farming|Farming XP]].
## Usage
### Crop Analyzer
A Chorus Fruit can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]] for 600,000 [[Coins]]. When analyzed, it grants 300 [[Copper]]. The first time analyzing a Chorus Fruit grants an additional 1,000 [[Copper]].
### Mutations
Chorus Fruit are used to create a [[Phantomleaf]] and [[Timestalk]].
## Gallery
- Chorus Fruit in the Greenhouse Chorus Fruit in the [[Greenhouse]]

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Feb 26, 2026|Fixed 2 or more Chorus Fruit teleporting onto the same block deleting all but 1.|

# Conclamatus Glyph
Categories: Epic items, Items, Pages with UIs
[Conclamatus Glyph]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Rift Transferrable ([[Rift Dimension]]): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GLYPH_CONCLAMATUS
Tooltip: A scroll ingrained with an ancient symbol.; If the pattern is mimicked with a powerful artifact, it may be possible to cast a Warding Spell.; EPIC
The Conclamatus Glyph is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Quest Item obtained in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Obtaining
The Conclamatus Glyph can be obtained after starting [[Sorcerer Okron]]'s quest and imbuing the [[Pernimius Glyph]], then opening the chest at [[Coordinates|-51.5, 73, -168.5]] in the barn within the [[Dreadfarm]]. Once given to Okron, it can no longer be obtained.
## Usage
The Conclamatus Glyph can be given to [[Sorcerer Okron]]. When given, he will task the player with using the [[Warding Diamathame]] to imbue a spell onto the house.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|You found Conclamatus Glyph! You should return it to Okron...|Finding the Glyph|
|You already have one in your inventory!|Trying to claim another Glyph while already owning one|

- The imbued particles surrounding the house

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Conclamatus Glyph.|

## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|1x [[Farm Crystal]] 64x [[Cornucopia]]|Recipe: [[Cornucopia]] x8 ×8 + [[Farm Crystal]] → Cornucopia Crystal|
## Usage
When placed on the [[Private Island]], a Cornucopia Crystal increases the speed of [[Farming]] [[Minions]] by 10%. Each unique [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] grants an additional 1% speed bonus. Only 1 Cornucopia Crystal can be placed at once. A Cornucopia Crystal cannot be placed together with a [[Farm Crystal]].

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: CORRUPT_SOIL
Tooltip: Minion Upgrade; This item can be used as a minion upgrade. Place it in any mob spawning minion and it will turn nearby mobs into their corrupted forms.; EPIC
The Corrupt Soil is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Minions|Minion Upgrade]] item that turns mobs from [[Minions|Mob-spawning Minions]] to their [[Corrupted Mobs|X Corrupted X]] forms.
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]])|20x [[Corrupted Fragment]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Mycelium]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x20 ×8 + [[Corrupted Fragment]] x20 → Corrupt Soil|
## Usage
### Minion Upgrade
Corrupt Soil can be placed in mob-spawning [[Minions]], see below. When placed in a minion, Corrupt Soil [[Corrupted Mobs|X Corrupts X]] all mobs spawned by the minion. This makes the mobs drop 1 [[Sulphur]] and 1 [[Corrupted Fragment]].
- Mob-spawning Minions
- [[Cow Minion]]
- [[Pig Minion]]
- [[Chicken Minion]]
- [[Sheep Minion]]
- [[Rabbit Minion]]
- [[Zombie Minion]]
- [[Magma Cube Minion]]
- [[Cave Spider Minion]]
- [[Ghast Minion]]
- [[Skeleton Minion]]
- [[Creeper Minion]]
- [[Spider Minion]]
- [[Blaze Minion]]
- [[Slime Minion]]
- [[Revenant Minion]]
- [[Tarantula Minion]]
- [[Enderman Minion]]
- [[Voidling Minion]]
- [[Inferno Minion]]
- [[Vampire Minion]]
## Bugs
- While the island is loaded, Corrupt Soil triggers for every successfully generated loot table entry of the main drop. Often this also results in 1 [[Sulphur]] and 1 [[Corrupted Fragment]], but for [[Revenant Minion]], [[Voidling Minion]], [[Skeleton Minion]], [[Tarantula Minion]], [[Magma Cube Minion]], and [[Slime Minion]] this results in a chance for multiple [[Sulphur]] and [[Corrupted Fragment]] drops.
  Show/Hide Examples
  [[Slime Minion]] has 3 loot table entries for [[Slimeball]]: 1 [[Slimeball]] with 100% (1 in 1), 1 [[Slimeball]] with 50% (1 in 2), 1 [[Slimeball]] with 50% (1 in 2). So [[Slime Minion]] can get 1–3 [[Sulphur]] and 1–3 [[Corrupted Fragment]] per kill, averaging to 2 (100% + 50% + 50%) of each. The amount of drops per loot table entry does not matter. For example, [[Revenant Minion]] spawns [[Revenant]] which has 3 loot table entries for [[Rotten Flesh]]: 2 [[Rotten Flesh]] with 100% (1 in 1) 1 [[Rotten Flesh]] with 50% (1 in 2) 2 [[Rotten Flesh]] with 33% (1 in 3.03) So [[Revenant Minion]] can get 1–3 [[Sulphur]] and 1–3 [[Corrupted Fragment]] per kill, averaging to 1.83 (100% + 50% + 33%) of each.
## Trivia
- This item shares the same texture as [[Potato Spreading]].

A Corrupted Fragment is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] crafting material obtained by slaying [[Corrupted Mobs]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Corrupted Fragments are dropped by slain [[Corrupted Mobs]].
### Minions
Corrupted Fragments are generated by [[Minions]] when a [[Corrupt Soil]] is applied to them.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Corrupted Bait]]|Magmafish V (5) ([[Magmafish]])|[[Corrupted Fragment]] 1x Corrupted Fragment 1x [[Magmafish]]|Recipe: Corrupted Fragment + [[Magmafish]] → [[Corrupted Bait]] x16|
|[[Totem of Corruption]]|Sulphur IV (4) ([[Sulphur]])|[[Corrupted Fragment]] 20x Corrupted Fragment 20x [[Enchanted Sulphur]] 60x [[Magma Chunk]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Sulphur]] x10 ×2 + [[Magma Chunk]] x30 ×2 + Corrupted Fragment x20 → [[Totem of Corruption]]|
|[[Corrupt Soil]]|Mycelium VI (6) ([[Mycelium]])|[[Corrupted Fragment]] 20x Corrupted Fragment 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) [[Enchanted Mycelium]]|Recipe: [[Enchanted Mycelium]] x20 ×8 + Corrupted Fragment x20 → [[Corrupt Soil]]|
|[[Enchanted Book]] (Corruption I ([[Corruption]]))|None|3x [[Enchanted Paper]] [[Corrupted Fragment]] 64x Corrupted Fragment|Recipe: [[Enchanted Paper]] ×3 + Corrupted Fragment x64 → [[Corruption]]|

### Light Levels
As the ability states, [[Defense]] and [[Intelligence]] of Crystal Armor depend on the light level the player is currently in. The armor also changes color depending on light levels. Full data can be seen below:
|Light Level|Stat percent|Color|
|-|-|-|
|0|12.5%|⁠ #1F0030|
|1|25%|⁠ #46085E|
|2|37.5%|⁠ #54146E|
|3|50%|⁠ #5D1C78|

|5|75%|⁠ #6A2C82|
|6|87.5%|⁠ #7E4196|
|7|100%|⁠ #8E51A6|
|8|112.5%|⁠ #9C64B3|
|9|125%|⁠ #A875BD|
|10|137.5%|⁠ #B88BC9|
|11|150%|⁠ #C9A3D4|
|12|162.5%|⁠ #D9C1E3|
|13|175%|⁠ #E5D1ED|
|14|187.5%|⁠ #EFE1F5|
|15|200%|⁠ #FCF3FF|
## Trivia
- The set bonus, Refraction, refers to a term in Physics referring to how light changes the direction it moves in when it passes from one medium to another.
- It was possible to dye any armor that has a custom color and leather texture by having this armor equiped going into the light level that gives you the color you wanted and then clicking many times in your armor slot with the piece you wanted to dye in your cursor. This has been already patched, however.

The Crystal Fragment is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] crafting material obtained from [[The End]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
Crystal Fragments have a 12.2% chance to drop from killing a [[Zealot]] holding an Ender Chest.
Additionally, earning 1st place in an [[End Stone Protector]] fight will grant [[Crystal Fragment]] 3 Crystal Fragment, and earning 2nd-5th place will reward [[Crystal Fragment]] 1 Crystal Fragment.
### Ender Crystals
Destroying [[Ender Crystal|Ender Crystals]] during an [[Ender Dragon]] fight will grant a Crystal Fragment.
## Usage
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Catalyst]]|End Stone IX (9) ([[End Stone]])|[[Crystal Fragment]] 1x Crystal Fragment 8x [[End Stone]]|Recipe: [[End Stone]] ×8 + Crystal Fragment → [[Catalyst]]|
|Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl IV (4) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x [[Enchanted Leather]] 4x [[Dragon Claw]] [[Crystal Fragment]] 8x Crystal Fragment|Recipe: Crystal Fragment x2 ×4 + [[Dragon Claw]] ×4 + [[Enchanted Leather]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
|Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl VI (6) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x Small Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]) 4x [[Summoning Eye]] [[Crystal Fragment]] 16x Crystal Fragment|Recipe: Crystal Fragment x4 ×4 + [[Summoning Eye]] ×4 + [[Dragon Sack]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
|Large Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]])|Ender Pearl IX (9) ([[Ender Pearl]])|1x Medium Dragon Sack ([[Dragon Sack]]) 4x [[Dragon Horn]] [[Crystal Fragment]] 32x Crystal Fragment|Recipe: Crystal Fragment x8 ×4 + [[Dragon Horn]] ×4 + [[Dragon Sack]] → [[Dragon Sack]]|
## Tips
- Crystal Fragments can easily be obtained by entering new [[The End]] lobbies until one fighting an [[Ender Dragon]] or [[End Stone Protector]] is found.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Crystal Fragment as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Aug 2, 2019|0.7|Added Crystal Fragment.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Crystal Fragment to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Crystal Fragment as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

Sell: 1,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DEATH_BOW
Tooltip: Gear Score: 340 (370); Damage: +300 (+330); Deals +100% damage to  Undead mobs.; Your arrows have a 50% chance to bounce to another target after it hits something.; This item can be reforged!; ❣ Requires The Catacombs Floor VI Completion.; EPIC DUNGEON BOW
The Death Bow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] [[Bows|Bow]].
## Obtaining
A Death Bow can be purchased from [[Ophelia]]'s shop for 5,000,000 [[Coins]] and a [[Super Undead Bow]] upon completion of [[The Catacombs - Floor VI]].
## Usage
Bow:
Death Bow [[Damage]]: +300
[Effects]
[Deals +100% damage to Undead (Undead (Mob Type)) mobs.; Your arrows have a 50% chance to bounce to another target after it hits something.]
The Death Bow, being a bow, can be used for ranged damage. It deals +100% more damage to [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs and grants a 50% chance for arrows to bounce to another target.
## Essence Crafting
Essence Required; [[Undead Essence]]:
|Action|Cost|Cumul. (Cumulative)|
|-|-|-|
|✪|40|40|
|✪✪|80|120|
|✪✪✪|150|270|
|✪✪✪✪|300; 10,000 [[Coins]]|570; 10,000 [[Coins]]|
|✪✪✪✪✪|600; 25,000 [[Coins]]|1,170; 35,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- A Death Bow can be used as a support item in higher floors. A common strategy involves shooting a separate mob with the [[Death Bow]] Death Bow due to its high single-hit damage: the Rapid Fire ultimate ability deals 75% of the highest damage dealt in the past minute, so this strategy helps the wielder deal incredibly high damage with the ability.
## Trivia
- The Death Bow was leaked in the [[Essence/List/Undead|Undead Essence]] section of the [[Essence Cost|Essence Guide]] before the release of [[The Catacombs - Floor V]].
- The Death Bow's passive could apply to the [[Bonemerang]] when swapping to it, dealing high bonus damage to Undead Mobs.

|[Lv157]|[[Phantom Fisher]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|1%|
|[Lv69]|[[Werewolf]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
### Reforging
Deep Sea Orb can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Submerged]] reforge, to grant [[Crit Chance]], [[Fishing Speed]], and [[Sea Creature Chance]], based on the armor's rarity. This has no [[Mining]] requirement and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece.
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+0.5 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +2 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +1 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.6 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +4 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +1 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.7 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +6 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +2 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.8 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +8 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +3 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+0.9 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +10 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +4 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +12 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +5 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|+1.1 SCC ([[Sea Creature Chance]]) +14 CC ([[Crit Chance]]) +6 FS ([[Fishing Speed]])|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|

The Defective Monitor is obtained by finding terminals, hacking them, and then choosing the correct color for [[Unhinged Kloon]]. The correct colors directly match the colors on the skin of [[Unhinged Kloon]] - a rainbow in correct order when looking towards the NPC.

|Necron's Chestplate ([[Necron's Armor]])|None|[[Diamante's Handle]] 8 Diamante's Handle Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Diamante's Handle ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]] → [[Necron's Armor]]|

|Necron's Boots ([[Necron's Armor]])|None|[[Diamante's Handle]] 8 Diamante's Handle Wither Boots ([[Wither Armor]]) variant|Recipe: Diamante's Handle ×8 + [[Wither Armor]]/[[Maxor's Armor]]/[[Storm's Armor]]/[[Goldor's Armor]] → [[Necron's Armor]]|
## Trivia
- Unlike other [[Precursor Relic|Precursor Relics]], Diamante's Handle was unstackable until version 0.26.
  - This was due to [[Horse Armor|Diamond Horse Armor]] not being stackable in vanilla Minecraft.

# Diamond Skin

[Diamond Skin]

The Diamond Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Diamond Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 3, 2024. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 1,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Diamond Skin can be applied to a [[Goblin Armor|Goblin Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

# Diamonite
Aliases: Fleet
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Dwarven Mines, Forged items
Magical form of diamond.
[Diamonite]
Type: Reforge Stone ([[Reforge Stones]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Reforge/Power Stone]
Reforge: Fleet
Applied To: Mining Tools ([[Tools]])
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XV (15) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 153,600x (2,400 stacks (153,600 items total)) [[Diamond]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DIAMONITE
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Fleet reforge when combined with a Mining Tool.; Magical form of diamond.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Mining Speed  Block Fortune; Fleet Bonus Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 15.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Diamonite is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Fleet]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]]. It grants [[Mining Speed]] and [[Block Fortune]].
## Obtaining
The Diamonite can be forged once the player has reached [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain II (2)]]. It requires 3 [[Refined Diamond|Refined Diamonds]] and 6 hours to forge.
### Forging Tree
- [[Diamonite]] 1 Diamonite
  - 3 [[Refined Diamond]] 6 [[Enchanted Diamond Block]] 960 (15 stacks) [[Enchanted Diamond]] 153,600 (2,400 stacks) [[Diamond]]
## Usage
### Reforging
Diamonite can be applied to [[Pickaxes]], [[Drills]] and the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Fleet]] reforge, to grant [[Mining Speed]] and [[Block Fortune]], which scale with the Mining Tool's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XV (15)]]. A Mining Tool that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Fleet]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Fleet Bonus, which grants [[Mining Spread]] on [[Blocks]].
|Stat Bonus|Rarity COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Rarity RARE ([[Rarity]])|Rarity EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Rarity MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|Rarity DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Stat Bonus|+25 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +10 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+30 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +20 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+35 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +30 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+45 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +40 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+55 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +50 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+65 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +75 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|+75 Blk Frt ([[Block Fortune]]) +100 M.Spd ([[Mining Speed]])|
|Reforge Bonus|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|Fleet Bonus; Grants +50 Mining Spread towards Blocks.|
|Cost to apply|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|250,000 [[Coins]]|
## Tips
- This is the best [[Reforging|Reforge]] when mining [[Blocks]], such as [[Obsidian]], [[Hard Stone]], [[Mycelium]], [[Red Sand]] or [[Ice]].
- It works best when paired with a set of [[Glossy Mineral Armor]], or regular [[Mineral Armor]] and [[Armadillo Pet]] ( [[Hard Stone]] specifically) or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Snail Pet]].

# Ditto Skin
Categories: Epic items, Items, Events, Bingo
[Ditto Skin]
Type: Cosmetic
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: Bingo Event ([[Bingo]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Merchants: Bingo ([[Bingo (NPC)]])
Buy: 100 Bingo Points ([[Bingo]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DITTO_SKIN
Tooltip: Spawn a NPC with the captured skin.; Ability: Copycat Copies the skin of an unsuspecting player.; Cooldown: 10s; * Co-op Soulbound * Requires Ⓑ Bingo Rank III!; EPIC COSMETIC
Ditto Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] Cosmetic that can copy another player's skin and spawn an [[Island NPC]] with the captured skin.
## Obtaining
A Ditto Skin can be purchased from the [[Bingo (NPC)|Bingo Shop]] for 100 [[Bingo|Bingo Points]], once the player has achieved Ⓑ Bingo Rank III (3).
## Usage
Cosmetic:
Ditto Skin
[Effects]
[Ability: Copycat; Copies the skin of an unsuspecting player.; Cooldown: 10s]
Hold this item and right-click on a player to copy their skin. It can be used multiple times.
Once used, the item becomes [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] to prevent abuse.
After capturing a skin, it can be placed on a player's [[Private Island]] to spawn an NPC with the captured skin.
## Trivia
- The name "Ditto" probably originates from the Pokémon of the same name, which is known for its ability to transform.
  - The simplistic face and color also somewhat resembles Ditto.
## See Also
- [[Ditto Blob]]
- [[Ditto Skull]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 31, 2024|Ditto Skin is no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 1, 2022|Added Ditto Skin.|
|Unknown Date|Changed the texture of the Ditto Skin.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Ditto Skin is no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) and can be traded and auctioned.|
|Aug 6, 2024|Ditto Skin is once again Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]).|
|Aug 6, 2024|Ditto Skin is no longer Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) by default once again.|
|Aug 6, 2024|Ditto Skin now becomes Co-op Soulbound ([[Soulbound]]) after it is used.|

Attacking an enemy ends the effect early.; You can Syphon this shard from your Hunting Box.; Right-click to send to Hunting Box!; Shift Right-click to move all!; ❣ Requires Hunting Skill 15.; EPIC FOREST SHARD (ID E43)
The Dive Ghast Shard is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Attributes|Shard]] that grants the [[Attributes|Ghastly Distraction]] [[Attributes|Attribute]].
## Obtaining
### Hunting
A Dive Ghast Shard can be obtained from [[Hunting]] a [[Dive Ghast]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] or enough [[Charm Chance]] to [[Charm]] it.
### Fusion
A Dive Ghast Shard can be obtained through [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]], specifically [[Attribute Fusion|ID Fusion]] and [[Attribute Fusion|Chameleon Fusion]].
||
||
[Loading...]
[Show More]
## Usage
### Syphoning
Being an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]], the Dive Ghast Shard can be added to the player's [[Attributes|Hunting Box]] and syphoned to level up the [[Attributes|Ghastly Distraction]] [[Attributes|Attribute]], which grants the following bonus:
- Ghastly Distraction ([[Attributes]]); Pacifies enemies for +1s–10s after mining Opal Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) in the [[Smoldering Tomb]] Does not work on Slayer Bosses, Slayer Minibosses. Attacking an enemy ends the effect early.
As more shards are syphoned, higher levels are reached, up to level 10. Each level up also grants [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 SkyBlock XP]].
|Level|Shards; Required|Total; Shards|Total; SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ (1)|1|1|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ (2)|1|2|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ (3)|2|4|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ (4)|2|6|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ (5)|3|9|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ (6)|3|12|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ (7)|4|16|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ (8)|4|20|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ (9)|5|25|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ (10)|7|32|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
### Fusion
A Dive Ghast Shard can be used in [[Attribute Fusion|Fusion]] to create other [[Attributes|Shards]].

## Obtaining
The Doge Plushie Wolf Skin could be purchased from the Summer 2024 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it cost 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]. However, due to being before the Winter 2024 Bundle, it cannot appear in future bundles, making it now unobtainable.

The Doge Plushie Wolf Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Wolf Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance.

# Dragon Horn
Aliases: Renowned
Categories: Epic items, Reforge stones, Crafting ingredients, Mob Drops

Drop Chance: 30%
