Applied To: [[Armor]]
Apply Cost: 250,000 [[Coins]]
Req. Skill Level: Mining XXV (25) ([[Mining]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✔ Yes
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Sell: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: DRAGON_HORN
Tooltip: Reforge Stone Combinable in Anvil; Applies the Renowned reforge when combined with Armor.; Grants the following stats that scale with the item's rarity:;  Health  Defense  Strength  Crit Chance  Crit Damage  Attack Speed  Intelligence  Speed; Renowned Bonus Grants +1%  Magic Find and +1% to ALL Combat Stats.; Right-click to view rarity scaling!; ❣ Requires Mining Skill 25.; EPIC REFORGE STONE
The Dragon Horn is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] that applies the [[Reforging|Renowned]] [[Reforging|Reforge]] to [[Armor]], granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Speed]].
## Obtaining
A Dragon Horn has a 30% base chance to drop from a [[Ender Dragon|Superior Dragon]] after reaching at least 448 [[Ender Dragon|Dragon Weight]].
## Usage
### Reforging
The Dragon Horn can be applied to [[Armor]] using an [[Anvil]] or [[The Hex]] to apply the [[Reforging|Renowned]] reforge, to grant [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Strength]], [[Crit Chance]], [[Crit Damage]], [[Attack Speed]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Speed]] which scale with the armor's rarity. This has a skill requirement of [[Mining|Mining XXV (25)]] and can be done with any armor, regardless of the piece or rarity. Armor that is already reforged to [[Reforging|Renowned]] will grant the stats, even if the player doesn't meet the requirement to apply it.
Additionally, it grants the Renowned Bonus, which increases all of the player's [[Stats|Combat Stats]] and [[Magic Find]] by 1%.

## Trivia
- The [[Dragon Scale]] as well as [[Dragon Claw]] and [[Dragon Horn]] Dragon Horn are most likely references to the Nintendo Switch game The Legend of Zelda: Breath of the Wild released in 2017, in which Link can shoot the three dragons Dinraal, Naydra and Farosh for item materials. Depending on his targeting of the claw, scale, or horn, he will receive that specific item. Likewise, in Hypixel SkyBlock, the three materials are ranked from worst to best: Scale, Claw, and Horn.
- The [[Reforging|Renowned]] reforge grants the same base stat values as the [[Reforging|Spiked]] reforge, but additionally increases all [[Stats|Combat Stats]] and [[Magic Find]] by 1%.
## Notes
- The Two-Headed Strike perk from the [[Essence Shops|Dragon Essence Shop]] increases the [[Attack Speed]] granted by [[Reforging|Renowned]] by 2 per tier of the perk, up to 10 [[Attack Speed]].

# Dragon Hydra Skin
Categories: Epic items, Helmet Skins, Taylor's Collection
[Dragon Hydra Skin]
Type: Helmet Skin ([[Helmet Skins]])
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: WATER_HYDRA_DRAGON
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Item skins give your gear a fresh new look! Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to Water Hydra Head.; EPIC COSMETIC
The Dragon Hydra Skin is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be purchased from [[Taylor|Taylor's Collection]].
## Obtaining
The Dragon Hydra Skin can be purchased from [[Taylor]] as part of her permanent collection. Being [[Rarity|EPIC]], it costs 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Dragon Hydra Skin can be applied to a [[Water Hydra Head]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance.

Tooltip: Damage: +120; Strength: +40; Crit Chance: +15%; Crit Damage: +50%; Shot Cooldown: 0.5s; Dragon Tracer V Arrows home towards dragons if they are within 10 blocks.; Some of your enchantments require a higher Enchanting level!; Ability: Dragon Stalker Deal 15% bonus damage to Dragons.; Shortbow: Instantly shoots!; This item can be reforged!; * Co-op Soulbound * EPIC BOW
The Dragon Shortbow is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Bows|Bow]]. It is automatically enchanted with [[Dragon Tracer|Dragon Tracer V]].
## Obtaining
A Dragon Shortbow has a chance of dropping from a [[Seer]] when the [[Lone Adventurer]] loses their Dragon Shortbow and the player has finished the [[Pearl Dealer]] questline.
After returning the Dragon Shortbow to the [[Lone Adventurer]], the player can talk to the [[Pearl Dealer]] to claim a Dragon Shortbow.
Once obtained, the player can purchase another Dragon Shortbow from [[Rusty]] for 100,000 [[Coins]].
## Usage
Bow:
Dragon Shortbow [[Damage]]: +120 [[Strength]]: +40 [[Crit Chance]]: +15 [[Crit Damage]]: +50
[Effects]
[Ability: Dragon Stalker; Deal 15% more bonus damage to Dragons.]
## Essence Crafting
This weapon can be upgraded with [[Dragon Essence]]. Once converted, it requires [[Catacombs|Catacombs VIII (8)]] to use while in the [[Catacombs]].
